## Their own words
- adze: lf141: my adze | the adze
- alehouse: cw165: the alehouse | the back room of the alehouse
- alley: sw123: the alley | our alley
- almsbook: cw208: the alms book | the almonry ledger
- almsdoor: cw208: the alms door | the almonry door
- amble: sm131: a long amble | a ramble that goes nowhere in particular
- anchor: cw176: an anchor | a wharf anchor | a great fat anchor
- anvil: lf152: the anvil | my anvil
- apple: cw178: an apple fresh off the cart | a hard red apple; cw180: a sharp green apple | an apple that bites back; cw191: a crisp apple | an apple that cracks like a knuckle; cw196: a sour little apple | an apple that makes your eyes water; cw203: a hard apple | an apple with a crack to it like ice; cw204: an apple | a good red apple; cw210: a crisp apple | an apple that snaps clean; fh109: a crisp apple | an apple that snaps when you bite it; lf102: a crisp apple | an apple with a snap to it; mb131: a hard green apple | an apple with a crack to it when you bite; rm102: a crisp apple | an apple that crunches; sm125: an apple that snaps | a hard green apple; sw118: a crisp apple | an apple that cracks when you bite it
- apples: fh108: winter apples | good keeping apples; lf156: the apples | a basket of windfalls; sw118: apples | my pocket apples
- apron: cw165: my apron | an apron stiff with it; cw186: my apron | the apron; cw200: my apron | the apron pocket; sm124: my apron | this old apron

## Opinions
- church: cw101 against 1: Praying never once got me #work#. The plate goes round faster than any help does. || cw166 for 1: The chapel fed this house through a #bad# month and asked nothing. I'll not hear the plate sneered a || cw168 for 1: When #shop# let my house go #hungry#, the chapel didn't. When #bells# go I'm in my pew, and I stay t || cw170 mixed 1: A candle goes up from me for every boat still out. I don't need a sermon to tell me why. || cw171 for 2: When my house had nothing, the chapel fed it. Every Restday since, I've had my seat, #dear#. || cw172 for 1: I like the chapel best when the sermon's done and everybody's gone home. Then it's #chapel#, and it' || cw173 for 1: When nobody else would, the chapel put bread on our table. That's on #tally#, and I'll be paying it  || cw174 mixed 0: I go in when it's #cold#. #whiff.capitalize# comes in with me and sits in the pew. || cw177 for 1: The chapel fed us when we'd nothing. I'll sit in its back pew as long as I've legs. #oath#. || cw180 mixed 0: I go when #bells# call and not much oftener. They do most of my praying for me. || cw181 mixed 0: I sell the chapel its #candles#. What they're lit for is between the lighter and the Bright One. || cw182 for 1: The chapel fed my house through a bad winter. That's #debt#, and I've #knot# for it. || cw184 for 1: When nobody had a crumb for us, the chapel did. A copper in their bowl, holed or not. || cw187 mixed 1: I like #chapel# better than a full one.
